      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-010",
        "concern": "Plan-hash CAS migration is broken: v2 strips plan_hash from current_run.json and proposes 'tail-seek + parse run_started \u2192 cache' as the replacement for gate.py:528. But run_started is the FIRST event in events.jsonl; tail-seek of the last 4KB cannot find it on any non-trivial run. The CAS must either scan the whole file (defeating the perf claim) or plan_hash must be stored in lease.json or current_run.json. Real correctness gap introduced by v2.",
        "resolution": "v3 addresses the five concrete correctness gaps critique_v2 found, plus three smaller refinements, all within v2's design shape. (1) plan_hash moves into lease.json (resolves FLAG-010 / correctness-1 \u2014 v2's 'parse run_started from tail' was broken because run_started is the FIRST event, not the tail; reading plan_hash inside the same flock the append uses is free). (2) GateDecision gains writer_epoch + run_dir + session_id fields so the four post-dispatch record_* helpers (record_dispatch_complete at gate.py:1099, plus the three phase-5-reserved helpers at 1186/1201/1211) can each open their own short WriterContext via a new writer_context_from_decision() helper \u2014 resolves FLAG-011 / correctness-2 / callers-3 / criteria_quality. (3) WriterContext auto-rebinds stale session.run_id on entry by re-reading current_run.json and updating both the in-memory session and the on-disk session file \u2014 resolves correctness-3 (Tab A attaches with run_id=None, Tab B starts, Tab A's next verb rebinds without error). (4) Role enum gains 'orphan-pending' for the present-but-unclaimed lease case, with a clear takeover hint printed by cmd_attach \u2014 resolves correctness-4 ('writer without claim' was dishonest). (5) inbox.py:216 decision='abort' now calls BOTH clear_current_run AND release_writer_lease (full clear, matching existing clear_active_run semantic) \u2014 resolves correctness-5 behavior regression. Plus: tail-seek chunked follow-back doubles window up to file size, no 64KB cap (correctness-7); verify_chain at gate.py:533 replaced with tail-only verify done inside append_event_locked (correctness-6); cmd_next reorders so session check precedes preamble print (all_locations-3); SKILL.md line 62 retained with a clarifying note that pack-level '--thread' is a variant-lineage identifier unrelated to the removed CLI verb (scope, all_locations-1, issue_hints-2). callers-2 (nested staleness in inbox\u2192gate_command) is documented as correct behavior in the WriterContext contract: epoch is a fence, not a freshness guarantee. issue_hints-1 (doctor gating) confirmed acceptable \u2014 first-run bootstrap is handled by attach/init. issue_hints-3 already addressed in v2."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-011",
        "concern": "WriterContext coverage gap: gate.py:1105 (record_dispatch_complete), 1186-1198 (record_step_attested), 1201-1208 (record_nested_entered), 1211-1218 (record_nested_exited) all call append_event from OUTSIDE gate_command. record_dispatch_complete is invoked from pipeline.main()'s `finally:` block at line 59, after _dispatch returns. _run_inline_checks (gate.py:1136, 1146, 1155, 1164) is called inside _dispatch_code/_dispatch_attested so it IS within gate_command \u2014 OK. But the four record_* helpers are out-of-band. Plan v2's 'wrap gate_command body in WriterContext' strategy does not reach them. The must-criterion 'every site in gate.py uses WriterContext' will fail unless GateDecision carries the writer epoch into the post-dispatch path or each record_* helper opens its own WriterContext from session resolution.",
        "resolution": "v3 addresses the five concrete correctness gaps critique_v2 found, plus three smaller refinements, all within v2's design shape. (1) plan_hash moves into lease.json (resolves FLAG-010 / correctness-1 \u2014 v2's 'parse run_started from tail' was broken because run_started is the FIRST event, not the tail; reading plan_hash inside the same flock the append uses is free). (2) GateDecision gains writer_epoch + run_dir + session_id fields so the four post-dispatch record_* helpers (record_dispatch_complete at gate.py:1099, plus the three phase-5-reserved helpers at 1186/1201/1211) can each open their own short WriterContext via a new writer_context_from_decision() helper \u2014 resolves FLAG-011 / correctness-2 / callers-3 / criteria_quality. (3) WriterContext auto-rebinds stale session.run_id on entry by re-reading current_run.json and updating both the in-memory session and the on-disk session file \u2014 resolves correctness-3 (Tab A attaches with run_id=None, Tab B starts, Tab A's next verb rebinds without error). (4) Role enum gains 'orphan-pending' for the present-but-unclaimed lease case, with a clear takeover hint printed by cmd_attach \u2014 resolves correctness-4 ('writer without claim' was dishonest). (5) inbox.py:216 decision='abort' now calls BOTH clear_current_run AND release_writer_lease (full clear, matching existing clear_active_run semantic) \u2014 resolves correctness-5 behavior regression. Plus: tail-seek chunked follow-back doubles window up to file size, no 64KB cap (correctness-7); verify_chain at gate.py:533 replaced with tail-only verify done inside append_event_locked (correctness-6); cmd_next reorders so session check precedes preamble print (all_locations-3); SKILL.md line 62 retained with a clarifying note that pack-level '--thread' is a variant-lineage identifier unrelated to the removed CLI verb (scope, all_locations-1, issue_hints-2). callers-2 (nested staleness in inbox\u2192gate_command) is documented as correct behavior in the WriterContext contract: epoch is a fence, not a freshness guarantee. issue_hints-1 (doctor gating) confirmed acceptable \u2014 first-run bootstrap is handled by attach/init. issue_hints-3 already addressed in v2."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-012",
        "concern": "Session.run_id drift vs current_run.json: cmd_attach with no current_run.json sets session.run_id=None. If another tab subsequently runs cmd_start, current_run.json is written but the first tab's session file is NOT updated. The first tab now has stale session.run_id=None while the project has an active run. Plan does not specify whether the first tab's verbs auto-rebind (re-read current_run.json) or error. Same multi-tab-state-divergence class of bug the sprint exists to fix.",
        "resolution": "Plan step 10 cmd_attach: 'if absent \u2192 run_id=None, role=writer (no run yet, ready for cmd_start).' Step 12 cmd_start: 'replace write_active_run(slug, run_id, plan_hash, \u2026) with two calls: write_current_run(slug, run_id) AND lease.write_lease_init(run_dir, session.id). Session's run_id is set + session file rewritten' \u2014 but only THIS session's file, not the other tab's."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-013",
        "concern": "cmd_attach 'present and unowned \u2192 role=writer, do NOT yet acquire the lease' creates a dead-end state: cmd_start refuses (current_run.json present), and only `astrid sessions takeover` can claim the orphan lease. Calling this state role=writer is misleading because the session cannot mutate. Either attach should auto-claim under the lease lock (incrementing epoch as a takeover would), or rename to role=orphan-pending so callers know they need explicit takeover.",
        "resolution": "Plan step 10 cmd_attach discovery rule: 'if present and unowned \u2192 role=writer, do NOT yet acquire the lease (lease is acquired by astrid start or by an explicit takeover).' Step 12 cmd_start guard rejects if current_run.json exists, so this writer can never start."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-014",
        "concern": "Inbox-abort semantic regression: current inbox.py:212-217 calls clear_active_run(slug, ...) on decision='abort' (full clear). Plan v2 step 12 inbox.py:216 bullet says only 'release_writer_lease(run_dir)' and explicitly 'clear_current_run is NOT called here.' But the abort decision should logically clear current_run.json too (the run IS aborted). Otherwise current_run.json points to an aborted run and `cmd_start` is blocked until manual cleanup. Likely a plan-author misread of existing semantics.",
        "resolution": "Existing inbox.py:212-217 calls clear_active_run for decision='abort'. Plan v2 step 12 inbox.py:216 bullet: 'inbox.py:216 (clear on consume): release_writer_lease(run_dir) (run continues but writer slot is freed). clear_current_run is NOT called here \u2014 consume-then-abort vs consume-then-continue is the existing semantic.'"
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-015",
        "concern": "verify_chain still called at gate.py:533 on every gate_command entry. Plan v2 retires verify_chain from the append path but leaves it in the gate path. Combined with the gate's 4KB tail-seek append, verify_chain becomes the bottleneck for any long-running plan (full O(n) chain re-validation on each verb invocation). Plan should either keep this site (acknowledging cost is per-verb not per-append) or migrate it to a lazy 'last-K events' check.",
        "resolution": "gate.py:533: `ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)`. Plan step 2 says 'Drop verify_chain from this path (kept as audit-only)' for the append helper but does not address the gate-entry site."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-016",
        "concern": "Tail-seek 64KB cap: plan v2 specifies '4KB window, expand to 64KB then fail loudly' for the seek-from-end tail read. Events that include user feedback or evidence strings could plausibly exceed 64KB (free-form text). Either expand the fallback to follow the file in larger chunks or document a hard limit on event size enforced at append time.",
        "resolution": "Plan v2 Step 2 bullet 'tail-seek read': 'If the 4KB window has no newline (single oversized line), expand to 64KB then fail loudly.' Events can carry `feedback` strings (lifecycle_ack.py:write_iteration_feedback)."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-018",
        "concern": "SKILL.md line 62 still documents `iteration_video.run inspect <thread>`, and iteration_video/run.py:40 still references `--thread @active`. Plan v2 removes only line 60 but leaves the iteration_video thread documentation intact. The plan's claim 'Remove every thread / @active / variant-selection / prefix-attribute reference from agent-facing prose' is therefore only partially achievable while threads/ remains in production use. Worth documenting that the iteration_video thread argument is a pack-internal concept that agents may still encounter.",
        "resolution": "SKILL.md:62 references `inspect <thread>` argument. iteration_video/run.py:40 reads `thread_ref` defaulting to '@active'. Plan step 18 only describes removing the line-60 paragraph."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-019",
        "concern": "Tail-only verify formula is incorrect: plan v3 step 2 reads 'recompute _event_hash(parsed_tail.prev_hash, parsed_tail) and compare with parsed_tail.hash.' But events.py stores only `hash` on each event \u2014 prev_hash is not a stored field; it's a parameter to _event_hash. To recompute the tail's hash you must read the second-to-last line's `hash` value (= prev_hash for the tail). As written, `parsed_tail.prev_hash` is None/AttributeError. Either drop tail-only verify (the CAS already guarantees integrity for the current append) or extend the tail-seek to also parse the second-to-last line.",
        "resolution": "v4 lands six concrete code-level fixes to v3 plus three smaller refinements: (1) DROP the broken tail-only chain verify formula (FLAG-019/correctness-1) \u2014 events.py doesn't store prev_hash; the StaleTailError CAS on tail_hash == expected_prev_hash is already sufficient integrity for the current append. Full-chain verify_chain stays callable for tests + audit; a future `astrid audit verify-chain` verb is deferred to Sprint 5a. (2) ADD writer-authorization check to WriterContext.__enter__ via new NotWriterError (FLAG-020/correctness-2/callers/criteria_quality-1) \u2014 `lease['attached_session_id'] != self.session.id` raises; readers and orphan-pending sessions cannot mutate. (3) DOCUMENT cmd_start write ordering: lease.json FIRST, then current_run.json (correctness-4/all_locations-2) \u2014 ensures auto-rebind readers never observe a current_run.json without a corresponding lease. Document in current_run.py and in Step 12 with explicit code comment. (4) SD-023 amendment (correctness-5/criteria_quality-2): preamble precedes operator-facing messages WITHIN a bound session; the 'no session bound' error is the explicit exception. Amend preamble.py top-of-file comment and lifecycle.py:407-411 comment. (5) Migration STOP-LINE on malformed active_run.json (scope-2): hard abort by default; opt-in `--force-skip-malformed` for explicit skip. (6) Auto-rebind side-effect on session file documented in WriterContext docstring AND in the `attached_session` fixture, which provides a `refresh()` helper (all_locations-1). Plus: discoverable TODO(sprint-N) marker in astrid/threads/__init__.py (scope-1); new writer-auth tests for reader and orphan-pending refusal (Step 14); new mid-chain corruption visibility tests (Step 3 + Step 19) confirm verify_chain still catches it offline (correctness-3). issue_hints/issue_hints-1 confirmed acceptable trade-offs."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-020",
        "concern": "WriterContext lacks writer-authorization check: plan v3 Step 9 captures lease['writer_epoch'] and lease['plan_hash'] but never verifies `lease['attached_session_id'] == self.session.id`. A reader or orphan-pending session can enter WriterContext, see the matching (unchanged) epoch, and successfully append events. The epoch CAS only catches takeovers (which bump epoch); it does NOT catch an unauthorized session that holds a stale role. Plan should add `if lease['attached_session_id'] != self.session.id: raise NotWriterError(...)` at __enter__.",
        "resolution": "v4 lands six concrete code-level fixes to v3 plus three smaller refinements: (1) DROP the broken tail-only chain verify formula (FLAG-019/correctness-1) \u2014 events.py doesn't store prev_hash; the StaleTailError CAS on tail_hash == expected_prev_hash is already sufficient integrity for the current append. Full-chain verify_chain stays callable for tests + audit; a future `astrid audit verify-chain` verb is deferred to Sprint 5a. (2) ADD writer-authorization check to WriterContext.__enter__ via new NotWriterError (FLAG-020/correctness-2/callers/criteria_quality-1) \u2014 `lease['attached_session_id'] != self.session.id` raises; readers and orphan-pending sessions cannot mutate. (3) DOCUMENT cmd_start write ordering: lease.json FIRST, then current_run.json (correctness-4/all_locations-2) \u2014 ensures auto-rebind readers never observe a current_run.json without a corresponding lease. Document in current_run.py and in Step 12 with explicit code comment. (4) SD-023 amendment (correctness-5/criteria_quality-2): preamble precedes operator-facing messages WITHIN a bound session; the 'no session bound' error is the explicit exception. Amend preamble.py top-of-file comment and lifecycle.py:407-411 comment. (5) Migration STOP-LINE on malformed active_run.json (scope-2): hard abort by default; opt-in `--force-skip-malformed` for explicit skip. (6) Auto-rebind side-effect on session file documented in WriterContext docstring AND in the `attached_session` fixture, which provides a `refresh()` helper (all_locations-1). Plus: discoverable TODO(sprint-N) marker in astrid/threads/__init__.py (scope-1); new writer-auth tests for reader and orphan-pending refusal (Step 14); new mid-chain corruption visibility tests (Step 3 + Step 19) confirm verify_chain still catches it offline (correctness-3). issue_hints/issue_hints-1 confirmed acceptable trade-offs."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-021",
        "concern": "cmd_start write ordering for current_run.json + lease.json visibility: plan v3 says cmd_start writes both files, but doesn't specify ordering. WriterContext auto-rebind reads current_run.json then lease.json outside any lock. If a tab observes current_run.json after Tab B's write but before lease.json is flushed, lease.json may not exist yet. Plan should specify: cmd_start writes lease.json FIRST (atomic), then current_run.json (atomic), so any reader that sees the new current_run.json is guaranteed to find the corresponding lease.json.",
        "resolution": "Plan v3 Step 12 lifecycle.py:201 bullet: 'write_current_run(slug, run_id) AND write_lease_init(...)' \u2014 ordering unspecified."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-022",
        "concern": "Tail-only verify is weaker than verify_chain: if events.jsonl is corrupted in the middle (e.g., disk error wrote zeros into line 50 of a 200-event log), tail-only verify at gate-entry won't detect it. Plan v3 says full-chain verify becomes an audit verb but does not list an acceptance criterion that anything other than the audit verb (which a user must explicitly invoke) catches mid-chain corruption. Document the trade-off or add a periodic full-verify.",
        "resolution": "Plan v3 Phase 1 Step 2: 'replaces verify_chain on the hot path AND for gate.py:533. Full-chain verify becomes an explicit audit verb.' No criterion for when full-chain verify is actually run."
      },
      {

Specifies stop-hook session-precedence and drops the projects-root scan (all_locations-1); historical AGENT.md files left alone (all_locations-2); adds docs/HOOKS.md + docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md to the doc sweep (all_locations-3). Verb functions take a `session` argument so tests bypass the gate by design (callers-1); `gate_command` gains a `writer_epoch` kwarg so inbox re-entry threads the outer epoch through (callers-2); `astrid author test --project` bypasses the session gate as a developer/CI path (callers-3). Narrows CLI allowlist to brief-canonical set plus `sessions detach` (issue_hints-1/3); removes `doctor`/`setup` from allowlist. Variant-selection migration becomes a no-op stderr audit since threads stay in place (issue_hints-2). Tightens `default_timeline_id` validation to slug-typed (criteria_quality-2). Adds literal breadcrumb output template for status test (criteria_quality-3). Adds new test file `test_current_run.py` + new module `astrid/core/project/current_run.py`.",

    "id": "FLAG-010",
    "concern": "Plan-hash CAS migration is broken: v2 strips plan_hash from current_run.json and proposes 'tail-seek + parse run_started \u2192 cache' as the replacement for gate.py:528.     "evidence": "v3 addresses the five concrete correctness gaps critique_v2 found, plus three smaller refinements, all within v2's design shape. (1) plan_hash moves into lease.json (resolves FLAG-010 / correctness-1 \u2014 v2's 'parse run_started from tail' was broken because run_started is the FIRST event, not the tail; reading plan_hash inside the same flock the append uses is free). (2) GateDecision gains writer_epoch + run_dir + session_id fields so the four post-dispatch record_* helpers (record_dispatch_complete at gate.py:1099, plus the three phase-5-reserved helpers at 1186/1201/1211) can each open their own short WriterContext via a new writer_context_from_decision() helper \u2014 resolves FLAG-011 / correctness-2 / callers-3 / criteria_quality. (3) WriterContext auto-rebinds stale session.run_id on entry by re-reading current_run.json and updating both the in-memory session and the on-disk session file \u2014 resolves correctness-3 (Tab A attaches with run_id=None, Tab B starts, Tab A's next verb rebinds without error). (4) Role enum gains 'orphan-pending' for the present-but-unclaimed lease case, with a clear takeover hint printed by cmd_attach \u2014 resolves correctness-4 ('writer without claim' was dishonest). (5) inbox.py:216 decision='abort' now calls BOTH clear_current_run AND release_writer_lease (full clear, matching existing clear_active_run semantic) \u2014 resolves correctness-5 behavior regression. Plus: tail-seek chunked follow-back doubles window up to file size, no 64KB cap (correctness-7); verify_chain at gate.py:533 replaced with tail-only verify done inside append_event_locked (correctness-6); cmd_next reorders so session check precedes preamble print (all_locations-3); SKILL.md line 62 retained with a clarifying note that pack-level '--thread' is a variant-lineage identifier unrelated to the removed CLI verb (scope, all_locations-1, issue_hints-2). callers-2 (nested staleness in inbox\u2192gate_command) is documented as correct behavior in the WriterContext contract: epoch is a fence, not a freshness guarantee. issue_hints-1 (doctor gating) confirmed acceptable \u2014 first-run bootstrap is handled by attach/init. issue_hints-3 already addressed in v2.",

        Requirements:
        - Produce structured JSON only.
        - `tasks` must be an ordered array of task objects. Every task object must include:
          - `id`: short stable task ID like `T1`
          - `description`: concrete work item
          - `depends_on`: array of earlier task IDs or `[]`
          - `status`: always `"pending"` at finalize time
          - `executor_notes`: always `""` at finalize time
          - `reviewer_verdict`: always `""` at finalize time
          - `kind`: indicating the type of work. One of:
            - `code`: writes or modifies source files (executor must produce `files_changed`).
            - `test`: writes or modifies test files, or runs the test suite (executor must produce `files_changed` in tests/ OR `commands_run` containing pytest/test invocations).
            - `audit`: read-only investigation — grep, code review, schema inspection (executor evidence is `executor_notes` describing findings; no `files_changed` expected).
            - `research`: external research or non-code investigation (executor evidence is `executor_notes`).
            - `docs`: writes documentation files (executor must produce `files_changed`).
            If unsure, default to `code`.
        - `watch_items` must be an array of strings covering runtime risks, critique concerns, and assumptions to keep visible during execution.
        - `sense_checks` must be an array with one verification question per task. Every sense-check object must include:
          - `id`: short stable ID like `SC1`
          - `task_id`: the related task ID
          - `question`: reviewer verification question
          - `verdict`: always `""` at finalize time
        - `user_actions` must be an array of human-only setup or operational actions. Use IDs `U1`, `U2`, ... and include `description` plus `phase` (`before_execute` or `after_execute`). Use optional `blocks_task_ids` when an action blocks specific tasks, optional `rationale` when useful, and `requires_human_only_reason` ONLY when the user_action is the sole coverage for a plan step.
- Include ONLY actions that require a human outside the executor's repo-editing work: env vars or secrets, infra access such as cloud accounts or VPN, DB migrations the human must trigger, manual UI/UX smoke tests, deploys, and out-of-band approvals.
- Anything that touches code in the repo MUST be a task, not a user_action. Reading docs, editing files, running tests, and writing migration SQL are tasks. Negative example: writing the migration SQL is a task, not a user_action.
- Positive examples: `U1: Set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY in .env (before_execute)`; `U2: Manually smoke test the production deploy in the browser (after_execute)`.
        - `meta_commentary` must be a single string with execution guidance, gotchas, or judgment calls that help the executor succeed.
        - `validation` must be an object that self-checks plan coverage:
          - `plan_steps_covered`: enumerate EVERY step from the approved plan. For each step, provide a short `plan_step_summary` (the step's intent in one phrase) and `finalize_item_ids` (array of task IDs `T*` or user_action IDs `U*` that implement or cover it — a single plan step may map to multiple tasks and/or user_actions).
          - `orphan_tasks`: task IDs that do not correspond to any plan step. Normally empty. If non-empty, explain in `completeness_notes`.
          - `completeness_notes`: free-text explanation of any gaps, deviations, or deliberate omissions.
          - `coverage_complete`: set to `true` only if every plan step has at least one finalize task or user_action AND you have verified the mapping by reviewing each entry. Set to `false` if any plan step is missing coverage.
          - Example:
          ```json
          "validation": {
            "plan_steps_covered": [
              {"plan_step_summary": "Add retry logic to API client", "finalize_item_ids": ["T1", "T2"]},
              {"plan_step_summary": "Set required production secret", "finalize_item_ids": ["U1"]}
            ],
            "orphan_tasks": [],
            "completeness_notes": "All plan steps mapped to tasks.",
            "coverage_complete": true
          }
          ```
        - Preserve information that strong existing artifacts already capture well: execution ordering, watch-outs, reviewer checkpoints, and practical context.
        - The structured output should be self-contained: an executor reading only `finalize.json` should have everything needed to work.
        - Keep the task count proportional to the work. A simple 1-2 file fix should be 2 tasks: (1) apply the fix, (2) run tests. Do NOT create separate "inspect" or "read" tasks for simple changes — the executor can read and fix in one step. Only create more tasks when the work has genuinely independent stages.
        - - The FINAL task MUST always be to run tests and verify the changes work. If specific test IDs or commands are mentioned in the original task, include them. Otherwise, the executor should find and run the tests most relevant to the files changed. If any test fails, read the error, fix the code, and re-run until they pass. Do NOT create new test files — run the project's existing test suite. Additionally, the executor should write a short throwaway script that reproduces the specific bug described in the task, run it to confirm the fix works, then delete the script.

    {
      "id": "T6",
      "description": "Implement WriterContext in `astrid/core/session/writer.py` with auto-rebind AND writer-authorization check. Define `NoRunBoundError(Exception)` LOCALLY in this module (NOT in events.py — it is a session-state condition, not an event-log error). __enter__: (1) read current_run.json; if on_disk_run_id != self.session.run_id, replace session via dataclasses.replace(self.session, run_id=on_disk_run_id, last_used_at=now()) AND rewrite session file via self.session.to_json(session_path(...)); (2) if session.run_id is None raise NoRunBoundError; (3) set self.run_dir = project_dir(self.session.project)/'runs'/self.session.run_id; (4) read lease; (5) WRITER-AUTH CHECK: if lease['attached_session_id'] != self.session.id raise NotWriterError(session_id=self.session.id, writer_id=lease['attached_session_id']); (6) capture self.expected_writer_epoch=lease['writer_epoch'], self.plan_hash=lease['plan_hash']. Provide an append(event) helper that calls append_event_locked with captured epoch and freshly-read tail (under flock). Provide `writer_context_from_decision(decision: GateDecision) -> WriterContext` factory that also performs the writer-auth check on entry. Docstring MUST explicitly note: 'On entry, WriterContext may rewrite the session file on disk if run_id changed via auto-rebind. Tests that snapshot the session file before invoking a verb must re-read after via the attached_session fixture's refresh() helper.' Tests at `tests/session/test_writer_context.py`: happy path, stale-epoch surfacing, stale-tail surfacing, test_reader_session_cannot_write (reader → NotWriterError, events.jsonl unchanged), test_orphan_pending_cannot_write (lease attached_session_id=None → NotWriterError, then takeover promotes), auto-rebind across run_id change.",

      "description": "Implement session CLI verbs in `astrid/core/session/cli.py`. `cmd_attach`: args parsing (project, --timeline, --session, --as agent:<id>); triggers identity bootstrap if missing; role assignment from current_run.json + lease — absent current_run.json → run_id=None,role=writer; present + lease.attached_session_id matches a different session → role=reader with takeover hint; present + lease.attached_session_id=null → role=orphan-pending with takeover hint spelling out run_id; prints `export ASTRID_SESSION_ID=<ulid>`. `cmd_sessions_ls`: list sessions with bound project+timeline+run+last-used. `cmd_sessions_detach [<id>]`: remove session file (no id = current tab). `cmd_sessions_takeover <run-id|session-id>`: orphan path → claim_orphan_lease (sets attached_session_id AND bumps epoch); live path → bump_epoch_and_swap_session; warm-target requires --force; both emit takeover events. `cmd_status`: literal breadcrumb template — bound prints session·agent·project·timeline·run·current step·last 5 events·inbox count·role; unbound + no identity → bootstrap; unbound + identity → list discoverable projects with explicit `astrid attach <slug>` command.   "watch_items": [
    "APEX CONTRACT (STOP-LINE): append_event_locked must hold ONE fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX) across tail-reread + epoch-reread + append + fsync. Splitting these is THE bug being fixed.",
    "DROP tail-only chain re-verification inside append_event_locked (FLAG-019). events.py does not store prev_hash on events; the StaleTailError CAS on tail_hash == expected_prev_hash IS the integrity gate. Do not 'helpfully' add the v3-style re-verify formula.",
    "NoRunBoundError lives in astrid/core/session/writer.py (NOT in events.py). It is a session-state condition. Step 9 pseudocode referenced it without declaring; gate warning #1 calls this out.",
    "bump_epoch_and_swap_session: when appending the takeover event via append_event_locked, pass expected_writer_epoch=NEW_EPOCH (N+1, post-bump). The lease has already been rewritten with N+1 by then.",
    "cmd_start write ordering: lease.json FIRST (atomic tmp+os.replace), THEN current_run.json (atomic tmp+os.replace). Inline code comment. Any reader observing the new current_run.json must be guaranteed to find a corresponding lease.json.",
    "WriterContext.__enter__ MUST perform the writer-auth check: if lease['attached_session_id'] != self.session.id raise NotWriterError. Apply uniformly to all 20 append entry paths including writer_context_from_decision.",
    "WriterContext auto-rebind SILENTLY rewrites the on-disk session file when run_id changes. Document in docstring. Tests that snapshot session file before a verb must call attached_session fixture's refresh() helper after.",
    "Legacy append_event wrapper combined with missing-lease defaults raises NotWriterError. Tests/migration paths MUST seed lease.json with attached_session_id=session.id before calling the wrapper. attached_session fixture seeds this implicitly.",
    "SD-023 amendment: PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE precedes every operator-facing message WITHIN A BOUND SESSION. 'no session bound' error is the documented exception. Update preamble.py top-of-file comment + lifecycle.py:407-411 comment.",
    "gate_command's writer_epoch kwarg (added in v3) is dead under the WriterContext-at-entry model. Prefer removing the kwarg; if kept, document as ignored.",
    "Migration --dry-run on malformed active_run.json should PREVIEW ('WOULD ABORT: malformed at <path>') and exit 0. Only --apply aborts non-zero. --force-skip-malformed opts into skip+audit.",
    "Removing verify_chain at gate.py:533 trades per-verb O(n) re-verification for tail-only CAS. Mid-chain corruption detection moves to offline audit. Document this as a known trade-off in acceptance criteria. Post-race verify_chain smoke is the in-loop check (Step 19.6).",
    "astrid/threads/ STAYS as an internal library (DEC-001). Only the user-facing `astrid thread` CLI verb is removed. Hype regression depends on threads/ surviving for orchestrator/runner.py + pack run.py callers.",
    "Subprocesses inheriting ASTRID_SESSION_ID is load-bearing (Sprint 0 env-inheritance spike confirms this). Do not silently scrub env anywhere new.",
    "Use astrid.core.project.jsonio.write_json_atomic for all new atomic JSON writes (recurring debt item). Do not roll a parallel atomic-write helper with different fsync semantics.",
    "Type hints everywhere; Pathlib over os.path. No comments unless the WHY is non-obvious."
